{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01UNKYnm6miwBZZ9Un72b4Ge","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I understand the image convention:\n\n```python\nIMAGE_CONVENTION_MAPPING = {\n    \"opengl\": 1,\n    \"opencv\": -1,\n}\n```\n\nAnd `macros.IMAGE_CONVENTION = \"opengl\"`, so `convention = 1`.\n\nIn the camera sensor:\n```python\nreturn img[::convention]  # img[::1] = img (no flip)\n```\n\nIf convention is `\"opengl\"`, `convention = 1`, so `img[::1]` = no change.\n\n\nIf convention is `\"opencv\"`, `convention = -1`, so `img[::-1]` = vertical flip.\n\nWith robosuite 1.4.1 using the default `\"opengl\"` convention, the image isn't flipped, meaning MuJoCo's OpenGL rendering (origin at bottom-left) returns the first row at the bottom of the scene. The OFT code then applies a double flip operation that converts from OpenGL's bottom-up convention to OpenCV's top-down convention while also flipping horizontally.\n\nBut I'm realizing that a double flip is actually a 180° rotation, not just a vertical correction—so if the image is only upside-down, I'd only need the vertical flip, and the horizontal flip would be an additional mirror. The real question is whether MuJoCo's rendering in the current robosuite version actually requires both flips, or if the OFT training data was collected with a specific orientation that the code is trying to match. If the robosuite version has changed how it renders images, the flip operation might need adjustment.\n\nLooking at the test results: disabling the flip gave correct approach direction but failed grasp, while enabling it reversed the direction entirely. This suggests the images might already be in the correct top-down orientation in robosuite 1.4.1, making the 180° rotation incorrect. I should check whether LIBERO's rendering environment sets its own image convention that might differ from the current {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01F7gqrpDDXP35WReKp3BkCe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"LIBERO's `ControlEnv` and its subclasses (`OffScreenRenderEnv`) don't change the `IMAGE_CONVENTION`. So robosuite's default `\"opengl\"` convention applies → `convention = 1` → `img[::1]` → no vertical flip.\n\nNow, with mujoco's native rendering, images come in OpenGL convention (origin at bottom-left, y-axis up). So the raw image has the bottom of the scene at row 0 and the top of the scene at the last row.\n\nWhen robosuite uses `img[::1]` (no flip), it returns the OpenGL image as-is.\n\n\nThe OFT code then applies a double flip operation that reverses both the row order and column order. This corrects the vertical orientation but introduces a left-right mirror effect. So with the flip enabled, the image is right-side up but horizontally mirrored, causing the robot to move in the opposite horizontal direction. Without the flip, the vertical orientation is inverted but the horizontal direction is correct—neither option produces a properly oriented image.\n\nNow I'm realizing the actual issue: a 180-degree rotation affects both axes, but the camera's perspective matters here. Since the agentview camera is positioned above and behind the table, flipping vertically changes the near/far perception in the image, while flipping horizontally reverses left/right. The robot's movements in the x-y plane depend on getting the horizontal orientation right, which is why disabling the flip showed correct behavior despite the upside-down image.\n\nLet me check what the OFT authors actually configured—they might have set a specific IMAGE_CONVENTION in robosuite or customized the macros differently than the default. I should look at the `get_libero_env` function to see if there's any special image handling setup {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MZqSCyzC8huAhAWmTvwgxj","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Utils for evaluating policies in LIBERO simulation environments.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport os\\\\n\\\\nimport imageio\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\n# import tensorflow as tf  # temporarily disabled\\\\nfrom libero.libero import get_libero_path\\\\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\\\n\\\\nfrom experiments.robot.robot_utils import (\\\\n    DATE,\\\\n    DATE_TIME,\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_libero_env(task, model_family, resolution=256):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Initializes and returns the LIBERO environment, along with the task description.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    task_description = task.language\\\\n    task_bddl_file = os.path.join(get_libero_path(\\\\\\\"bddl_files\\\\\\\"), task.problem_folder, task.bddl_file)\\\\n    env_args = {\\\\\\\"bddl_file_name\\\\\\\": task_bddl_file, \\\\\\\"camera_heights\\\\\\\": resolution, \\\\\\\"camera_widths\\\\\\\": resolution}\\\\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\\\n    env.seed(0)  # IMPORTANT: seed seems to affect object positions even when using fixed initial state\\\\n    return env, task_description\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_libero_dummy_action(model_family: str):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Get dummy/no-op action, used to roll out the simulation while the robot does nothing.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    return [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extracts third-person image from observations and preprocesses it.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    img = obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    img = img[::-1, ::-1]\\\\n    return img\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extracts wrist camera image from observations and preprocesses it.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    img = obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    img = img[::-1, ::-1]\\\\n    return img\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef save_rollout_video(rollout_images, idx, success, task_description, log_file=None):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Saves an MP4 replay of an episode.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    rollout_dir = f\\\\\\\"./rollouts/{DATE}\\\\\\\"\\\\n    os.makedirs(rollout_dir, exist_ok=True)\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.35}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20edf378-2c29-4db1-9f30-77d38d22d300","uuid":"2d0a29a0-ca81-4611-9fbc-545cd731dbb3","timestamp":"2026-05-22T13:42:01.545Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Utils for evaluating policies in LIBERO simulation environments.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport os\\\\n\\\\nimport imageio\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\n# import tensorflow as tf  # temporarily disabled\\\\nfrom libero.libero import get_libero_path\\\\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\\\n\\\\nfrom experiments.robot.robot_utils import (\\\\n    DATE,\\\\n    DATE_TIME,\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_libero_env(task, model_family, resolution=256):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Initializes and returns the LIBERO environment, along with the task description.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    task_description = task.language\\\\n    task_bddl_file = os.path.join(get_libero_path(\\\\\\\"bddl_files\\\\\\\"), task.problem_folder, task.bddl_file)\\\\n    env_args = {\\\\\\\"bddl_file_name\\\\\\\": task_bddl_file, \\\\\\\"camera_heights\\\\\\\": resolution, \\\\\\\"camera_widths\\\\\\\": resolution}\\\\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\\\n    env.seed(0)  # IMPORTANT: seed seems to affect object positions even when using fixed initial state\\\\n    return env, task_description\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_libero_dummy_action(model_family: str):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Get dummy/no-op action, used to roll out the simulation while the robot does nothing.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    return [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extracts third-person image from observations and preprocesses it.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    img = obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    img = img[::-1, ::-1]\\\\n    return img\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extracts wrist camera image from observations and preprocesses it.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    img = obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    img = img[::-1, ::-1]\\\\n    return img\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef save_rollout_video(rollout_images, idx, success, task_description, log_file=None):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Saves an MP4 replay of an episode.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    rollout_dir = f\\\\\\\"./rollouts/{DATE}\\\\\\\"\\\\n    os.makedirs(rollout_dir, exist_ok=True)\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.35}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Utils for evaluating policies in LIBERO simulation environments.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport math\\nimport os\\n\\nimport imageio\\nimport numpy as np\\n# import tensorflow as tf  # temporarily disabled\\nfrom libero.libero import get_libero_path\\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\n\\nfrom experiments.robot.robot_utils import (\\n    DATE,\\n    DATE_TIME,\\n)\\n\\n\\ndef get_libero_env(task, model_family, resolution=256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Initializes and returns the LIBERO environment, along with the task description.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    task_description = task.language\\n    task_bddl_file = os.path.join(get_libero_path(\\\"bddl_files\\\"), task.problem_folder, task.bddl_file)\\n    env_args = {\\\"bddl_file_name\\\": task_bddl_file, \\\"camera_heights\\\": resolution, \\\"camera_widths\\\": resolution}\\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\n    env.seed(0)  # IMPORTANT: seed seems to affect object positions even when using fixed initial state\\n    return env, task_description\\n\\n\\ndef get_libero_dummy_action(model_family: str):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Get dummy/no-op action, used to roll out the simulation while the robot does nothing.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1]\\n\\n\\ndef get_libero_image(obs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extracts third-person image from observations and preprocesses it.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    img = obs[\\\"agentview_image\\\"]\\n    img = img[::-1, ::-1]\\n    return img\\n\\n\\ndef get_libero_wrist_image(obs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extracts wrist camera image from observations and preprocesses it.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    img = obs[\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\"]\\n    img = img[::-1, ::-1]\\n    return img\\n\\n\\ndef save_rollout_video(rollout_images, idx, success, task_description, log_file=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Saves an MP4 replay of an episode.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    rollout_dir = f\\\"./rollouts/{DATE}\\\"\\n    os.makedirs(rollout_dir, exist_ok=True)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01LFV5d2u1rnMMFruFK7hstw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"The `get_libero_env` function creates an `OffScreenRenderEnv` with default parameters — no macros override for IMAGE_CONVENTION. So it uses the default `\"opengl\"` convention → `convention = 1` → no vertical flip by robosuite.\n\nThe OFT code then applies `[::-1, ::-1]` (180° rotation). \n\nNow, the key question: with the OFT authors' setup (robosuite 1.4.1, same as we have now), the image processing chain is:\n\n\n1. mujoco renders image in OpenGL convention (origin bottom-left)\n2. robosuite returns image as-is (opengl convention, step=1)\n3. OFT code does `[::-1, ::-1]` — 180° rotation\n\nThis means the OFT training data and the eval code are CONSISTENT — they both apply the same `[::-1, ::-1]` to the OpenGL-convention images. So the model was trained expecting 180° rotated OpenGL images.\n\nSo the flip should stay ON with robosuite 1.4.1 (opengl convention). But when I tested with robosuite 1.4.0 + flip ON, the robot moved in the wrong direction. This suggests robosuite 1.4.0 might have had different image handling behavior, which would explain the inconsistency between what the model expects and what we're actually feeding it.\n\nI'm also noticing there could be conflicting LIBERO installations — the editable install from the git repo (version 0.1.0) and the pip-installed version (0.1.1) which explicitly requires robosuite==1.4.0. This version mismatch might be causing unexpected behavior with how images are being processed.\n\nBefore diving deeper into macro file configurations, I should wait for TensorFlow to finish installing and then actually test whether the flip works with robosuite 1.4.1. But I'm also wondering if the OFT authors had a private macros file that set IMAGE_CONVENTION to \"opencv\" instead of the default \"opengl\" — that would explain the discrepancy, since robosuite is warning about no private macro file being found.\n\nRather than keep guessing at combinations, let me systematically test all four possible flip permutations: no flip, vertical flip only, horizontal flip only, and both flips together. I'll save the raw environment image and compare it against each flipped version to see which one actually looks correct.\n\nLooking back at the test results, the no-flip case showed correct directional movement but failed to grasp, while the double-flip case moved in the wrong direction entirely. This suggests the answer might be a single flip in one direction rather than both or neither. Let me trace through what happened with the no-flip case more carefully—the robot started at one position and moved toward the soup location, but I need to see where it actually ended up at frame 60 to understand what went wrong. I'm wondering if the rendering API itself changed between mujoco versions—the older Python bindings used OpenGL with bottom-up coordinates, but the newer 3.8.1 bindings might handle rendering differently. Since robosuite 1.4.1 relies on the new mujoco python bindings, I should trace through how robosuite wraps the mujoco 3.x renderer to see if that's where the coordinate discrepancy self.scn.flags[mujoco.mjtRndFlag.mjRND_IDCOLOR] = 0\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def read_pixels(self, width, height, depth=False, segmentation=False):\\\\r\\\\n        viewport = mujoco.MjrRect(0, 0, The image has the first row at the bottom of the viewport.\n\nThen in robosuite's `MjSim.render()` (the higher-level one), the image is returned with `[::convention]` applied. \n\nWith `convention=1` (opengl), there's no flip applied, but with `convention=-1` (opencv), it applies a vertical flip. We know that when flip is OFF (no mirror), the robot moves in the correct direction, which suggests the model wasn't trained on mirrored images. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RC6967eHxj7fkWJcgkTQZp","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"    def step(self, with_udd=True):\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Step simulation.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        mujoco.mj_step(self.model._model, self.data._data)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def render(\\\\r\\\\n        self,\\\\r\\\\n        width=None,\\\\r\\\\n        height=None,\\\\r\\\\n        *,\\\\r\\\\n        camera_name=None,\\\\r\\\\n        depth=False,\\\\r\\\\n        mode=\\\\\\\"offscreen\\\\\\\",\\\\r\\\\n        device_id=-1,\\\\r\\\\n        segmentation=False,\\\\r\\\\n    ):\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        Renders view from a camera and returns image as an `numpy.ndarray`.\\\\r\\\\n        Args:\\\\r\\\\n        - width (int): desired image width.\\\\r\\\\n        - height (int): desired image height.\\\\r\\\\n        - camera_name (str): name of camera in model. If None, the free\\\\r\\\\n            camera will be used.\\\\r\\\\n        - depth (bool): if True, also return depth buffer\\\\r\\\\n        - device (int): device to use for rendering (only for GPU-backed\\\\r\\\\n            rendering).\\\\r\\\\n        Returns:\\\\r\\\\n        - rgb (uint8 array): image buffer from camera\\\\r\\\\n        - depth (float array): depth buffer from camera (only returned\\\\r\\\\n            if depth=True)\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        if camera_name is None:\\\\r\\\\n            camera_id = None\\\\r\\\\n        else:\\\\r\\\\n            camera_id = self.model.camera_name2id(camera_name)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n        assert mode == \\\\\\\"offscreen\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"only offscreen supported for now\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        assert self._render_context_offscreen is not None\\\\r\\\\n        with _MjSim_render_lock:\\\\r\\\\n            self._render_context_offscreen.render(\\\\r\\\\n                width=width, height=height, camera_id=camera_id, segmentation=segmentation\\\\r\\\\n            )\\\\r\\\\n            return self._render_context_offscreen.read_pixels(width, height, depth=depth, segmentation=segmentation)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def add_render_context(self, render_context):\\\\r\\\\n        assert render_context.offscreen\\\\r\\\\n        if self._render_context_offscreen is not None:\\\\r\\\\n            # free context\\\\r\\\\n            del self._render_context_offscreen\\\\r\\\\n        self._render_context_offscreen = render_context\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def get_state(self):\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return MjSimState instance for current state.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        return MjSimState(\\\\r\\\\n            time=self.data.time,\\\\r\\\\n            qpos=np.copy(self.data.qpos),\\\\r\\\\n            qvel=np.copy(self.data.qvel),\\\\r\\\\n        )\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def set_state(self, value):\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        Set internal state from MjSimState instance. Should\\\\r\\\\n        call @forward afterwards to synchronize derived quantities.\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.time = value.time\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.qpos[:] = np.copy(value.qpos)\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.qvel[:] = np.copy(value.qvel)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def set_state_from_flattened(self, value):\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        Set internal mujoco state using flat mjstate array. Should\\\\r\\\\n        call @forward afterwards to synchronize derived quantities.\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n        See https://github.com/openai/mujoco-py/blob/4830435a169c1f3e3b5f9b58a7c3d9c39bdf4acb/mujoco_py/mjsimstate.pyx#L54\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        state = MjSimState.from_flattened(value, self)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n        # do this instead of @set_state to avoid extra copy of qpos and qvel\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.time = state.time\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.qpos[:] = state.qpos\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.qvel[:] = state.qvel\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def free(self):\\\\r\\\\n        # clean up here to prevent memory leaks\\\\r\\\\n        del self._render_context_offscreen\\\\r\\\\n        del self.data\\\\r\\\\n        del self.model\\\\r\\\\n        del self\\\\r\\\\n        gc.collect()\\\\r\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.36}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20edf378-2c29-4db1-9f30-77d38d22d300","uuid":"9c978fac-f6ce-4e3f-bf1e-86eb09ba5bec","timestamp":"2026-05-22T13:43:21.372Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"    def step(self, with_udd=True):\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Step simulation.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        mujoco.mj_step(self.model._model, self.data._data)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def render(\\\\r\\\\n        self,\\\\r\\\\n        width=None,\\\\r\\\\n        height=None,\\\\r\\\\n        *,\\\\r\\\\n        camera_name=None,\\\\r\\\\n        depth=False,\\\\r\\\\n        mode=\\\\\\\"offscreen\\\\\\\",\\\\r\\\\n        device_id=-1,\\\\r\\\\n        segmentation=False,\\\\r\\\\n    ):\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        Renders view from a camera and returns image as an `numpy.ndarray`.\\\\r\\\\n        Args:\\\\r\\\\n        - Should\\\\r\\\\n        call @forward afterwards to synchronize derived quantities.\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n        See https://github.com/openai/mujoco-py/blob/4830435a169c1f3e3b5f9b58a7c3d9c39bdf4acb/mujoco_py/mjsimstate.pyx#L54\\\\r\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n        state = MjSimState.from_flattened(value, self)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n        # do this instead of @set_state to avoid extra copy of qpos and qvel\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.time = state.time\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.qpos[:] = state.qpos\\\\r\\\\n        self.data.qvel[:] = state.qvel\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def free(self):\\\\r\\\\n        # clean up here to prevent memory leaks\\\\r\\\\n        del self._render_context_offscreen\\\\r\\\\n        del self.data\\\\r\\\\n        del self.model\\\\r\\\\n        del self\\\\r\\\\n        gc.collect()\\\\r\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.36}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"    def step(self, with_udd=True):\\r\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Step simulation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\r\\n        mujoco.mj_step(self.model._model, self.data._data)\\r\\n\\r\\n    def render(\\r\\n        self,\\r\\n        width=None,\\r\\n        height=None,\\r\\n        *,\\r\\n        camera_name=None,\\r\\n        depth=False,\\r\\n        mode=\\\"offscreen\\\",\\r\\n        device_id=-1,\\r\\n        segmentation=False,\\r\\n    ):\\r\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\r\\n        Renders view from a camera and returns image as an `numpy.ndarray`.\\r\\n        Args:\\r\\n        - width (int): desired image width.\\r\\n        - height (int): desired image height.\\r\\n        - camera_name (str): name of camera in model. If None, the free\\r\\n            camera will be used.\\r\\n        - depth (bool): if True, also return depth buffer\\r\\n        - device (int): device to use for rendering (only for GPU-backed\\r\\n            rendering).\\r\\n        Returns:\\r\\n        - rgb (uint8 array): image buffer from camera\\r\\n        - depth (float array): depth buffer from camera (only returned\\r\\n            if depth=True)\\r\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\r\\n        if camera_name is None:\\r\\n            camera_id = None\\r\\n        else:\\r\\n            camera_id = self.model.camera_name2id(camera_name)\\r\\n\\r\\n        assert mode == \\\"offscreen\\\", \\\"only offscreen supported for now\\\"\\r\\n        assert self._render_context_offscreen is not None\\r\\n        with _MjSim_render_lock:\\r\\n            self._render_context_offscreen.render(\\r\\n                width=width, height=height, camera_id=camera_id, segmentation=segmentation\\r\\n            )\\r\\n            return self._render_context_offscreen.read_pixels(width, height, depth=depth, segmentation=segmentation)\\r\\n\\r\\n    def add_render_context(self, render_context):\\r\\n        assert render_context.offscreen\\r\\n        if self._render_context_offscreen is not None:\\r\\n            # free context\\r\\n            del self._render_context_offscreen\\r\\n        self._render_context_offscreen = render_context\\r\\n\\r\\n    def get_state(self):\\r\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return MjSimState instance for current state.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\r\\n        return MjSimState(\\r\\n            time=self.data.time,\\r\\n            qpos=np.copy(self.data.qpos),\\r\\n            qvel=np.copy(self.data.qvel),\\r\\n        )\\r\\n\\r\\n    def set_state(self, value):\\r\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\r\\n        Set internal state from MjSimState instance. Should\\r\\n        call @forward afterwards to synchronize derived quantities.\\r\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\r\\n        self.data.time = value.time\\r\\n        self.data.qpos[:] = np.copy(value.qpos)\\r\\n        self.data.qvel[:] = np.copy(value.qvel)\\r\\n\\r\\n    def set_state_from_flattened(self, value):\\r\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\r\\n        Set internal mujoco state using flat mjstate array. Should\\r\\n        call @forward afterwards to synchronize derived quantities.\\r\\n\\r\\n        See https://github.com/openai/mujoco-py/blob/4830435a169c1f3e3b5f9b58a7c3d9c39bdf4acb/mujoco_py/mjsimstate.pyx#L54\\r\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\r\\n        state = MjSimState.from_flattened(value, self)\\r\\n\\r\\n        # do this instead of @set_state to avoid extra copy of qpos and qvel\\r\\n        self.data.time = state.time\\r\\n        self.data.qpos[:] = state.qpos\\r\\n        self.data.qvel[:] = state.qvel\\r\\n\\r\\n    def free(self):\\r\\n        # clean up here to prevent memory leaks\\r\\n        del self._render_context_offscreen\\r\\n        del self.data\\r\\n        del self.model\\r\\n        del self\\r\\n        gc.collect()\\r\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}}}

OFT code applies a 180° rotation via row and column reversal\n\nThis means the model receives a bottom-up image that's been rotated 180°, which is different from simply flipping it to top-down orientation—the rotation reverses both the row and column order, placing what was originally the top-left corner at the bottom-right.\n\nThe result is a left-right mirrored image with the correct vertical orientation. If the model was trained on this exact transformation, it should work correctly, but we're seeing the opposite behavior. This suggests either the model wasn't trained with this transformation, or there's a mismatch in how the training data was collected—possibly from a different robosuite version or camera convention. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CCYiPkbmxeKxw7ea4MQzDQ","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"def center_crop_image(image: Union[np.ndarray, Image.Image]) -> Image.Image:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Center crop an image to match training data distribution.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        image: Input image (PIL or numpy array)\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        Image.Image: Cropped PIL Image\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    batch_size = 1\\\\n    crop_scale = 0.9\\\\n\\\\n    # Convert to TF Tensor if needed\\\\n    if not isinstance(image, tf.Tensor):\\\\n        image = tf.convert_to_tensor(np.array(image))\\\\n\\\\n    orig_dtype = image.dtype\\\\n\\\\n    # Convert to float32 in range [0,1]\\\\n    image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, tf.float32)\\\\n\\\\n    # Apply center crop and resize\\\\n    image = crop_and_resize(image, crop_scale, batch_size)\\\\n\\\\n    # Convert back to original data type\\\\n    image = tf.clip_by_value(image, 0, 1)\\\\n    image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, orig_dtype, saturate=True)\\\\n\\\\n    # Convert to PIL Image\\\\n    return Image.fromarray(image.numpy()).convert(\\\\\\\"RGB\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef check_image_format(image: Any) -> None:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Validate input image format.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        image: Image to check\\\\n\\\\n    Raises:\\\\n        AssertionError: If image format is invalid\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    is_numpy_array = isinstance(image, np.ndarray)\\\\n    has_correct_shape = len(image.shape) == 3 and image.shape[-1] == 3\\\\n    has_correct_dtype = image.dtype == np.uint8\\\\n\\\\n    assert is_numpy_array and has_correct_shape and has_correct_dtype, (\\\\n        \\\\\\\"Incorrect image format detected! Make sure that the input image is a \\\\\\\"\\\\n        \\\\\\\"numpy array with shape (H, W, 3) and dtype np.uint8!\\\\\\\"\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef normalize_proprio(proprio: np.ndarray, norm_stats: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Normalize proprioception data to match training distribution.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        proprio: Raw proprioception data\\\\n        norm_stats: Normalization statistics\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        np.ndarray: Normalized proprioception data\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if ACTION_PROPRIO_NORMALIZATION_TYPE == NormalizationType.BOUNDS:\\\\n        mask = norm_stats.get(\\\\\\\"mask\\\\\\\", np.ones_like(norm_stats[\\\\\\\"min\\\\\\\"], dtype=bool))\\\\n        proprio_high, proprio_low = np.array(norm_stats[\\\\\\\"max\\\\\\\"]), np.array(norm_stats[\\\\\\\"min\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    elif ACTION_PROPRIO_NORMALIZATION_TYPE == NormalizationType.BOUNDS_Q99:\\\\n        mask = norm_stats.get(\\\\\\\"mask\\\\\\\", np.ones_like(norm_stats[\\\\\\\"q01\\\\\\\"], dtype=bool))\\\\n        proprio_high, proprio_low = np.array(norm_stats[\\\\\\\"q99\\\\\\\"]), np.array(norm_stats[\\\\\\\"q01\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    else:\\\\n        raise ValueError(\\\\\\\"Unsupported action/proprio normalization type detected!\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    normalized_proprio = np.clip(\\\\n        np.where(\\\\n            mask,\\\\n            2 * (proprio - proprio_low) / (proprio_high - proprio_low + 1e-8) - 1,\\\\n            proprio,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        a_min=-1.0,\\\\n        a_max=1.0,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    return normalized_proprio\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef prepare_images_for_vla(images: List[np.ndarray], cfg: Any) -> List[Image.Image]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Prepare images for VLA input by resizing and cropping as needed.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        images: List of input images as numpy arrays\\\\n        cfg: Configuration object with parameters\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        List[Image.Image]: Processed images ready for the model\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    processed_images = []\\\\n\\\\n    for image in images:\\\\n        # Validate format\\\\n        check_image_format(image)\\\\n\\\\n        # Resize if needed\\\\n        if image.shape != (OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE, OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE, 3):\\\\n            image = resize_image_for_policy(image, OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE)\\\\n\\\\n        # Convert to PIL image\\\\n        pil_image = Image.fromarray(image).convert(\\\\\\\"RGB\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        # Apply center crop if configured\\\\n        if cfg.center_crop:\\\\n            pil_image = center_crop_image(pil_image)\\\\n\\\\n        processed_images.append(pil_image)\\\\n\\\\n    return processed_images\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_vla_action(\\\\n    cfg: Any,\\\\n    vla: torch.nn.Module,\\\\n    processor: Any,\\\\n    obs: Dict[str, Any],\\\\n    task_label: str,\\\\n    action_head: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\\\n    proprio_projector: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\\\n    noisy_action_projector: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\\\n    use_film: bool = False,\\\\n) -> List[np.ndarray]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Generate action predictions with the VLA policy.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        cfg: Configuration object with parameters\\\\n        vla: The VLA model\\\\n        processor: Model processor for inputs\\\\n        obs: Observation dictionary\\\\n        task_label: Text description of the task\\\\n        action_head: Optional action head for continuous actions\\\\n        proprio_projector: Optional proprioception projector\\\\n        noisy_action_projector: Optional noisy action projector for diffusion\\\\n        use_film: Whether to use FiLM\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        List[np.ndarray]: Predicted actions\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    with torch.inference_mode():\\\\n\\\\n        # Collect all input images\\\\n        all_images = [obs[\\\\\\\"full_image\\\\\\\"]]\\\\n        if cfg.num_images_in_input > 1:\\\\n            all_images.extend([obs[k] for k in obs.keys() if \\\\\\\"wrist\\\\\\\" in k])\\\\n\\\\n        # Process images\\\\n        all_images = prepare_images_for_vla(all_images, cfg)\\\\n\\\\n        # Extract primary image and additional images\\\\n        primary_image = all_images.pop(0)\\\\n\\\\n        # Build VLA prompt\\\\n        prompt = f\\\\\\\"In: What action should the robot take to {task_label.lower()}?\\\\\\\\nOut:\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n        # Process primary image\\\\n        inputs = processor(prompt, primary_image).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\\\\n\\\\n        # Process additional wrist images if any\\\\n        if all_images:\\\\n            all_wrist_inputs = [\\\\n                processor(prompt, image_wrist).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16) for image_wrist in all_images\\\\n            ]\\\\n            # Concatenate all images\\\\n            primary_pixel_values = inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            all_wrist_pixel_values = [wrist_inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"] for wrist_inputs in all_wrist_inputs]\\\\n            inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"] = torch.cat([primary_pixel_values] + all_wrist_pixel_values, dim=1)\\\\n\\\\n        # Process proprioception data if used\\\\n        proprio = None\\\\n        if cfg.use_proprio:\\\\n            proprio = obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            proprio_norm_stats = vla.norm_stats[cfg.unnorm_key][\\\\\\\"proprio\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"] = normalize_proprio(proprio, proprio_norm_stats)\\\\n            proprio = obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Generate action\\\\n        if action_head is None:\\\\n            # Standard VLA output (single-image inputs, discrete actions)\\\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(**inputs, unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key, do_sample=False)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            # Custom action head for continuous actions\\\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(\\\\n                **inputs,\\\\n                unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key,\\\\n                do_sample=False,\\\\n                proprio=proprio,\\\\n                proprio_projector=proprio_projector,\\\\n                noisy_action_projector=noisy_action_projector,\\\\n                action_head=action_head,\\\\n                use_film=use_film,\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    # Return action chunk as list of actions\\\\n    return [action[i] for i in range(len(action))]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_action_from_server(\\\\n    observation: Dict[str, Any], server_endpoint: str = \\\\\\\"http://0.0.0.0:8777/act\\\\\\\"\\\\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Get VLA action from remote inference server.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        observation: Observation data to send to server\\\\n        server_endpoint: URL of the inference server\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        Dict[str, Any]: Action response from server\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    response = requests.post(\\\\n        server_endpoint,\\\\n 0.36}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"def center_crop_image(image: Union[np.ndarray, Image.Image]) -> Image.Image:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Center crop an image to match training data distribution.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        image: Input image (PIL or numpy array)\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Image.Image: Cropped PIL Image\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    batch_size = 1\\n    crop_scale = 0.9\\n\\n    # Convert to TF Tensor if needed\\n    if not isinstance(image, tf.Tensor):\\n        image = tf.convert_to_tensor(np.array(image))\\n\\n    orig_dtype = image.dtype\\n\\n    # Convert to float32 in range [0,1]\\n    image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, tf.float32)\\n\\n    # Apply center crop and resize\\n    image = crop_and_resize(image, crop_scale, batch_size)\\n\\n    # Convert back to original data type\\n    image = tf.clip_by_value(image, 0, 1)\\n    image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, orig_dtype, saturate=True)\\n\\n    # Convert to PIL Image\\n    return Image.fromarray(image.numpy()).convert(\\\"RGB\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef check_image_format(image: Any) -> None:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Validate input image format.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        image: Image to check\\n\\n    Raises:\\n        AssertionError: If image format is invalid\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    is_numpy_array = isinstance(image, np.ndarray)\\n    has_correct_shape = len(image.shape) == 3 and image.shape[-1] == 3\\n    has_correct_dtype = image.dtype == np.uint8\\n\\n    assert is_numpy_array and has_correct_shape and has_correct_dtype, (\\n        \\\"Incorrect image format detected! Make sure that the input image is a \\\"\\n        \\\"numpy array with shape (H, W, 3) and dtype np.uint8!\\\"\\n    )\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_proprio(proprio: np.ndarray, norm_stats: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Normalize proprioception data to match training distribution.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        proprio: Raw proprioception data\\n        norm_stats: Normalization statistics\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        np.ndarray: Normalized proprioception data\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if ACTION_PROPRIO_NORMALIZATION_TYPE == NormalizationType.BOUNDS:\\n        mask = norm_stats.get(\\\"mask\\\", np.ones_like(norm_stats[\\\"min\\\"], dtype=bool))\\n        proprio_high, proprio_low = np.array(norm_stats[\\\"max\\\"]), np.array(norm_stats[\\\"min\\\"])\\n    elif ACTION_PROPRIO_NORMALIZATION_TYPE == NormalizationType.BOUNDS_Q99:\\n        mask = norm_stats.get(\\\"mask\\\", np.ones_like(norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"], dtype=bool))\\n        proprio_high, proprio_low = np.array(norm_stats[\\\"q99\\\"]), np.array(norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"])\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported action/proprio normalization type detected!\\\")\\n\\n    normalized_proprio = np.clip(\\n        np.where(\\n            mask,\\n            2 * (proprio - proprio_low) / (proprio_high - proprio_low + 1e-8) - 1,\\n            proprio,\\n        ),\\n        a_min=-1.0,\\n        a_max=1.0,\\n    )\\n\\n    return normalized_proprio\\n\\n\\ndef prepare_images_for_vla(images: List[np.ndarray], cfg: Any) -> List[Image.Image]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Prepare images for VLA input by resizing and cropping as needed.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        images: List of input images as numpy arrays\\n        cfg: Configuration object with parameters\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List[Image.Image]: Processed images ready for the model\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    processed_images = []\\n\\n    for image in images:\\n        # Validate format\\n        check_image_format(image)\\n\\n        # Resize if needed\\n        if image.shape != (OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE, OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE, 3):\\n            image = resize_image_for_policy(image, OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE)\\n\\n        # Convert to PIL image\\n        pil_image = Image.fromarray(image).convert(\\\"RGB\\\")\\n\\n        # Apply center crop if configured\\n        if cfg.center_crop:\\n            pil_image = center_crop_image(pil_image)\\n\\n        processed_images.append(pil_image)\\n\\n    return processed_images\\n\\n\\ndef get_vla_action(\\n    cfg: Any,\\n    vla: torch.nn.Module,\\n    processor: Any,\\n    obs: Dict[str, Any],\\n    task_label: str,\\n    action_head: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\n    proprio_projector: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\n    noisy_action_projector: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\n    use_film: bool = False,\\n) -> List[np.ndarray]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Generate action predictions with the VLA policy.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        cfg: Configuration object with parameters\\n        vla: The VLA model\\n        processor: Model processor for inputs\\n        obs: Observation dictionary\\n        task_label: Text description of the task\\n        action_head: Optional action head for continuous actions\\n        proprio_projector: Optional proprioception projector\\n        noisy_action_projector: Optional noisy action projector for diffusion\\n        use_film: Whether to use FiLM\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List[np.ndarray]: Predicted actions\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    with torch.inference_mode():\\n\\n        # Collect all input images\\n        all_images = [obs[\\\"full_image\\\"]]\\n        if cfg.num_images_in_input > 1:\\n            all_images.extend([obs[k] for k in obs.keys() if \\\"wrist\\\" in k])\\n\\n        # Process images\\n        all_images = prepare_images_for_vla(all_images, cfg)\\n\\n        # Extract primary image and additional images\\n        primary_image = all_images.pop(0)\\n\\n        # Build VLA prompt\\n        prompt = f\\\"In: What action should the robot take to {task_label.lower()}?\\\\nOut:\\\"\\n\\n        # Process primary image\\n        inputs = processor(prompt, primary_image).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\\n\\n        # Process additional wrist images if any\\n        if all_images:\\n            all_wrist_inputs = [\\n                processor(prompt, image_wrist).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16) for image_wrist in all_images\\n            ]\\n            # Concatenate all images\\n            primary_pixel_values = inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"]\\n            all_wrist_pixel_values = [wrist_inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"] for wrist_inputs in all_wrist_inputs]\\n            inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"] = torch.cat([primary_pixel_values] + all_wrist_pixel_values, dim=1)\\n\\n        # Process proprioception data if used\\n        proprio = None\\n        if cfg.use_proprio:\\n            proprio = obs[\\\"state\\\"]\\n            proprio_norm_stats = vla.norm_stats[cfg.unnorm_key][\\\"proprio\\\"]\\n            obs[\\\"state\\\"] = normalize_proprio(proprio, proprio_norm_stats)\\n            proprio = obs[\\\"state\\\"]\\n\\n        # Generate action\\n        if action_head is None:\\n            # Standard VLA output (single-image inputs, discrete actions)\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(**inputs, unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key, do_sample=False)\\n        else:\\n            # Custom action head for continuous actions\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(\\n                **inputs,\\n                unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key,\\n                do_sample=False,\\n                proprio=proprio,\\n                proprio_projector=proprio_projector,\\n                noisy_action_projector=noisy_action_projector,\\n                action_head=action_head,\\n                use_film=use_film,\\n            )\\n\\n    # Return action chunk as list of actions\\n    return [action[i] for i in range(len(action))]\\n\\n\\ndef get_action_from_server(\\n    observation: Dict[str, Any], server_endpoint: str = \\\"http://0.0.0.0:8777/act\\\"\\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Get VLA action from remote inference server.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        observation: Observation data to send to server\\n        server_endpoint: URL of the inference server\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Dict[str, Any]: Action response from server\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    response = requests.post(\\n        server_endpoint,\\n        json=observation,\\n    )\\n    return response.json()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}}}

Resize using the same pipeline as in RLDS dataset builder\\\\n    img = tf.image.encode_jpeg(img)  # Encode as JPEG\\\\n    img = tf.io.decode_image(img, expand_animations=False, dtype=tf.uint8)  # Decode back\\\\n    img = tf.image.resize(img, resize_size, method=\\\\\\\"lanczos3\\\\\\\", antialias=True)\\\\n    img = tf.cast(tf.clip_by_value(tf.round(img), 0, 255), tf.uint8)\\\\n\\\\n    return img.numpy()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef crop_and_resize(image: tf.Tensor, crop_scale: float, batch_size: int) -> tf.Tensor:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Center-crop an image and resize it back to original dimensions.\\\\n\\\\n    Uses the same logic as in the training data pipeline for distribution matching.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        image: TF Tensor of shape (batch_size, H, W, C) or (H, W, C) with values in [0,1]\\\\n        crop_scale: Area of center crop relative to original image\\\\n        batch_size: Batch size\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        tf.Tensor: The cropped and resized image\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    # Handle 3D inputs by adding batch dimension if needed\\\\n    assert image.shape.ndims in (3, 4), \\\\\\\"Image must be 3D or 4D tensor\\\\\\\"\\\\n    expanded_dims = False\\\\n    if image.shape.ndims == 3:\\\\n        image = tf.expand_dims(image, axis=0)\\\\n        expanded_dims = True\\\\n\\\\n    # Calculate crop dimensions (note: we use sqrt(crop_scale) for h/w)\\\\n    new_heights = tf.reshape(tf.clip_by_value(tf.sqrt(crop_scale), 0, 1), shape=(batch_size,))\\\\n    new_widths = tf.reshape(tf.clip_by_value(tf.sqrt(crop_scale), 0, 1), shape=(batch_size,))\\\\n\\\\n    # Create bounding box for the crop\\\\n    height_offsets = (1 - new_heights) / 2\\\\n    width_offsets = (1 - new_widths) / 2\\\\n    bounding_boxes = tf.stack(\\\\n        [\\\\n            height_offsets,\\\\n            width_offsets,\\\\n            height_offsets + new_heights,\\\\n            width_offsets + new_widths,\\\\n        ],\\\\n        axis=1,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    # Apply crop and resize\\\\n    image = tf.image.crop_and_resize(\\\\n        image, bounding_boxes, tf.range(batch_size), (OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE, OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE)\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    # Remove batch dimension if it was added\\\\n    if expanded_dims:\\\\n        image = image[0]\\\\n\\\\n    return image\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef center_crop_image(image: Union[np.ndarray, Image.Image]) -> Image.Image:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Center crop an image to match training data distribution.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        image: Input image (PIL or numpy array)\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        Image.Image: Cropped PIL Image\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    batch_size = 1\\\\n    crop_scale = 0.9\\\\n\\\\n    # Convert to TF Tensor if needed\\\\n    if not isinstance(image, tf.Tensor):\\\\n        image = tf.convert_to_tensor(np.array(image))\\\\n\\\\n    orig_dtype = image.dtype\\\\n\\\\n    # Convert to float32 in range [0,1]\\\\n    image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, tf.float32)\\\\n\\\\n    # Apply center crop and resize\\\\n    image = crop_and_resize(image, crop_scale, batch_size)\\\\n\\\\n    # Convert back to original data type\\\\n    image = tf.clip_by_value(image, 0, 1)\\\\n    image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, orig_dtype, saturate=True)\\\\n\\\\n    # Convert to PIL Image\\\\n    return Image.fromarray(image.numpy()).convert(\\\\\\\"RGB\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef check_image_format(image: Any) -> None:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Validate input image format.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        image: Image to check\\\\n\\\\n    Raises:\\\\n        AssertionError: If image format is invalid\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    is_numpy_array = isinstance(image, np.ndarray)\\\\n    has_correct_shape = len(image.shape) == 3 and image.shape[-1] == 3\\\\n    has_correct_dtype = image.dtype == np.uint8\\\\n\\\\n    assert is_numpy_array and has_correct_shape and has_co\\\\n\\\\n... Process additional wrist images if any\\\\n        if all_images:\\\\n            all_wrist_inputs = [\\\\n                processor(prompt, image_wrist).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16) for image_wrist in all_images\\\\n            ]\\\\n            # Concatenate all images\\\\n            primary_pixel_values = inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            all_wrist_pixel_values = [wrist_inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"] for wrist_inputs in all_wrist_inputs]\\\\n            inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"] = torch.cat([primary_pixel_values] + all_wrist_pixel_values, dim=1)\\\\n\\\\n        # Process proprioception data if used\\\\n        proprio = None\\\\n        if cfg.use_proprio:\\\\n            proprio = obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            proprio_norm_stats = vla.norm_stats[cfg.unnorm_key][\\\\\\\"proprio\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"] = normalize_proprio(proprio, proprio_norm_stats)\\\\n            proprio = obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Generate action\\\\n        if action_head is None:\\\\n            # Standard VLA output (single-image inputs, discrete actions)\\\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(**inputs, unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key, do_sample=False)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            # Custom action head for continuous actions\\\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(\\\\n                **inputs,\\\\n                unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key,\\\\n                do_sample=False,\\\\n                proprio=proprio,\\\\n                proprio_projector=proprio_projector,\\\\n                noisy_action_projector=noisy_action_projector,\\\\n                action_head=action_head,\\\\n                use_film=use_film,\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    # Collect all input images\\n        all_images = [obs[\\\"full_image\\\"]]\\n        if cfg.num_images_in_input > 1:\\n            all_images.extend([obs[k] for k in obs.keys() if \\\"wrist\\\" in k])\\n\\n        # Process images\\n        all_images = prepare_images_for_vla(all_images, cfg)\\n\\n        # Extract primary image and additional images\\n        primary_image = all_images.pop(0)\\n\\n        # Build VLA prompt\\n        prompt = f\\\"In: What action should the robot take to {task_label.lower()}?\\\\nOut:\\\"\\n\\n        # Process primary image\\n        inputs = processor(prompt, primary_image).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\\n\\n        # Process additional wrist images if any\\n        if all_images:\\n            all_wrist_inputs = [\\n                processor(prompt, image_wrist).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16) for image_wrist in all_images\\n            ]\\n            # Concatenate all images\\n            primary_pixel_values = inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"]\\n            all_wrist_pixel_values = [wrist_inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"] for wrist_inputs in all_wrist_inputs]\\n            inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"] = torch.cat([primary_pixel_values] + all_wrist_pixel_values, dim=1)\\n\\n        # Process proprioception data if used\\n        proprio = None\\n        if cfg.use_proprio:\\n            proprio = obs[\\\"state\\\"]\\n            proprio_norm_stats = vla.norm_stats[cfg.unnorm_key][\\\"proprio\\\"]\\n            obs[\\\"state\\\"] = normalize_proprio(proprio, proprio_norm_stats)\\n            proprio = obs[\\\"state\\\"]\\n\\n        # Generate action\\n        if action_head is None:\\n            # Standard VLA output (single-image inputs, discrete actions)\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(**inputs, unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key, do_sample=False)\\n        else:\\n            # Custom action head for continuous actions\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(\\n                **inputs,\\n                unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key,\\n                do_sample=False,\\n                proprio=proprio,\\n                proprio_projector=proprio_projector,\\n                noisy_action_projector=noisy_action_projector,\\n                action_head=action_head,\\n                use_film=use_film,\\n            )\\n\\n    # Return action chunk as list of actions\\n    return [action[i] for i in range(len(action))]\\n\\n\\ndef get_action_from_server(\\n    observation: Dict[str, Any], server_endpoint: str = \\\"http://0.0.0.0:8777/act\\\"\\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Get VLA action from remote inference server.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        observation: Observation data to send to server\\n        server_endpoint: URL of the inference server\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Dict[str, Any]: Action response from server\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    response = requests.post(\\n        server_endpoint,\\n        json=observation,\\n    )\\n    return response.json()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01YPQ6vMgLkRPJsXAxZsFYzz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Q4eUHqg3yJCpZXTP1hX1er","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"bjb2-40593","command":"cd /root/autodl-tmp/openvla-oft-official && python3 -c \"\nimport re\n\nwith open('experiments/robot/openvla_utils.py', 'r') as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\n# Replace 'import tensorflow as tf' with a try/except fallback\nold_import = 'import tensorflow as tf'\nnew_import = '''try:\n    import tensorflow as tf\n    HAS_TF = True\nexcept ImportError:\n    HAS_TF = False'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(old_import, new_import)\n\n# Replace resize_image_for_policy\nold_resize = '''def resize_image_for_policy(img: np.ndarray, resize_size: Union[int, Tuple[int, int]]) -> np.ndarray:\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\n    Resize an image to match the policy's expected input size.\n\n    Uses the same resizing scheme as in the training data pipeline for distribution matching.\n\n    Args:\n        img: Numpy array containing the image\n        resize_size: Target size as int (square) or (height, width) tuple\n\n    Returns:\n        np.ndarray: The resized image\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\n    assert isinstance(resize_size, int) or isinstance(resize_size, tuple)\n    if isinstance(resize_size, int):\n        resize_size = (resize_size, resize_size)\n\n    # Resize using the same pipeline as in RLDS dataset builder\n    img = tf.image.encode_jpeg(img)  # Encode as JPEG\n    img = tf.io.decode_image(img, expand_animations=False, dtype=tf.uint8)  # Decode back\n    img = tf.image.resize(img, resize_size, method=\\\"lanczos3\\\", antialias=True)\n    img = tf.cast(tf.clip_by_value(tf.round(img), 0, 255), tf.uint8)\n\n    return img.numpy()'''\n\nnew_resize = '''def resize_image_for_policy(img: np.ndarray, resize_size: Union[int, Tuple[int, int]]) -> np.ndarray:\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Resize image matching training pipeline (JPEG roundtrip + lanczos3).\\\"\\\"\\\"\n    assert isinstance(resize_size, int) or isinstance(resize_size, tuple)\n    if isinstance(resize_size, int):\n        resize_size = (resize_size, resize_size)\n    if HAS_TF:\n        img = tf.image.encode_jpeg(img)\n        img = tf.io.decode_image(img, expand_animations=False, dtype=tf.uint8)\n        img = tf.image.resize(img, resize_size, method=\\\"lanczos3\\\", antialias=True)\n        img = tf.cast(tf.clip_by_value(tf.round(img), 0, 255), tf.uint8)\n        return img.numpy()\n    else:\n        import io\n        pil_img = Image.fromarray(img)\n        buf = io.BytesIO()\n        pil_img.save(buf, format=\\\"JPEG\\\")\n        buf.seek(0)\n        pil_img = Image.open(buf)\n        pil_img = pil_img.resize((resize_size[1], resize_size[0]), Image.LANCZOS)\n        return np.array(pil_img)'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(old_resize, new_resize)\n\n# Replace center_crop_image \nold_center = '''def center_crop_image(image: Union[np.ndarray, Image.Image]) -> Image.Image:\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\n    Center crop an image to match training data distribution.\n\n    Args:\n        image: Input image (PIL or numpy array)\n\n    Returns:\n        Image.Image: Cropped PIL Image\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\n    batch_size = 1\n    crop_scale = 0.9\n\n    # Convert to TF Tensor if needed\n    if not isinstance(image, tf.Tensor):\n        image = tf.convert_to_tensor(np.array(image))\n\n    orig_dtype = image.dtype\n\n    # Convert to float32 in range [0,1]\n    image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, tf.float32)\n\n    # Apply center crop and resize\n    image = crop_and_resize(image, crop_scale, batch_size)\n\n    # Convert back to original data type\n    image = tf.clip_by_value(image, 0, 1)\n    image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, orig_dtype, saturate=True)\n\n    # Convert to PIL Image\n    return Image.fromarray(image.numpy()).convert(\\\"RGB\\\")'''\n\nnew_center = '''def center_crop_image(image: Union[np.ndarray, Image.Image]) -> Image.Image:\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Center crop image (90% area) matching training pipeline.\\\"\\\"\\\"\n    crop_scale = 0.9\n    if HAS_TF:\n        batch_size = 1\n        if not isinstance(image, tf.Tensor):\n            image = tf.convert_to_tensor(np.array(image))\n        orig_dtype = image.dtype\n        image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, tf.float32)\n        image = crop_and_resize(image, crop_scale, batch_size)\n        image = tf.clip_by_value(image, 0, 1)\n        image = tf.image.convert_image_dtype(image, orig_dtype, saturate=True)\n        return Image.fromarray(image.numpy()).convert(\\\"RGB\\\")\n    else:\n        import math\n        if isinstance(image, np.ndarray):\n            image = Image.fromarray(image)\n        w, h = image.size\n        s = math.sqrt(crop_scale)\n        new_w, new_h = int(w * s), int(h * s)\n        left = (w - new_w) // 2\n        top = (h - new_h) // 2\n        image = image.crop((left, top, left + new_w, top + new_h))\n        image = image.resize((OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE, OPENVLA_IMAGE_SIZE), Image.LANCZOS)\n        return image.convert(\\\"RGB\\\")'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(old_center, new_center)\n\nwith open('experiments/robot/openvla_utils.py', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\n\nprint('OK — replaced TF functions with fallbacks')\n\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":514,"cache_read_input_tokens":96878,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":514},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20edf378-2c29-4db1-9f30-77d38d22d300","uuid":"0d28f759-1f98-4b5c-94dc-9b151bf51b4d"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01HSacjZzxCvmjWGKMhuNT4Y","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WjjM5gFwhTyKxuxKgGgR3Z","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"bjb2-40593","command":"cat > /tmp/test_vflip.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Test vertical-flip-only with robosuite 1.4.1: 80 steps diagnostic.\"\"\"\nimport sys, os\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-oft-official\")\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\nos.environ[\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\"] = \"0\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport robosuite, mujoco\nprint(f\"robosuite={robosuite.__version__}, mujoco={mujoco.__version__}\")\n\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark\nfrom experiments.robot.libero.libero_utils import get_libero_env, get_libero_image, get_libero_wrist_image\nfrom experiments.robot.openvla_utils import get_vla, get_processor, get_action_head, get_proprio_projector\nfrom experiments.robot.openvla_utils import get_vla_action, prepare_images_for_vla, normalize_proprio\nfrom experiments.robot.robot_utils import normalize_gripper_action, invert_gripper_action\n\n# Check current flip code\nimport inspect\nsrc = inspect.getsource(get_libero_image)\nprint(f\"get_libero_image source:\\n{src}\")\n\n# Config\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n@dataclass\nclass Cfg:\n    pretrained_checkpoint: str = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/openvla-oft/object\"\n    model_family: str = \"openvla\"\n    use_l1_regression: bool = True\n    use_diffusion: bool = False\n    use_film: bool = False\n    num_images_in_input: int = 2\n    use_proprio: bool = True\n    center_crop: bool = True\n    num_open_loop_steps: int = 8\n    lora_rank: int = 32\n    load_in_8bit: bool = False\n    load_in_4bit: bool = False\n    unnorm_key: str = \"\"\n    task_suite_name: str = \"libero_object\"\n\ncfg = Cfg()\n\n# Setup env\nbenchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\ntask_suite = benchmark_dict[\"libero_object\"]()\ntask = task_suite.get_task(0)\ninit_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(0)\ntask_description = task.language\nprint(f\"Task: {task_description}\")\n\nenv, _ = get_libero_env(task, \"openvla\", resolution=224)\nenv.seed(0)\nenv.reset()\nobs = env.set_init_state(init_states[0])\n\n# Load model\nvla = get_vla(cfg)\nprocessor = get_processor(cfg)\n\n# Load action head and proprio projector\nllm_dim = vla.config.text_config.hidden_size\naction_head = get_action_head(cfg, llm_dim)\nproprio_projector = get_proprio_projector(cfg, llm_dim, proprio_dim=8)\n\n# Get unnorm_key\nunnorm_key = None\nfor key in vla.norm_stats:\n    if \"libero_object\" in key:\n        unnorm_key = key\n        break\ncfg.unnorm_key = unnorm_key\nprint(f\"unnorm_key: {unnorm_key}\")\n\n# Helper: build obs dict\nfrom experiments.robot.libero.run_libero_eval import prepare_observation\nfrom experiments.robot.libero.run_libero_eval import process_action\n\nee_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\nprint(f\"Start EE: {ee_pos}\")\n\n# Run 80 steps (10 chunks)\nfor chunk_i in range(10):\n    step = chunk_i * 8\n    obs_dict = prepare_observation(obs, task_description)\n    \n    actions = get_vla_action(\n        cfg, vla, processor, obs_dict, task_description,\n        action_head=action_head,\n        proprio_projector=proprio_projector,\n    )\n    \n    for action in actions:\n        action = process_action(action)\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\n    \n    ee_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\n    grip = obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"]\n    print(f\"t={step+8:3d}: EE={np.array2string(ee_pos, precision=3, suppress_small=True)}, grip={np.array2string(grip, precision=3)}, done={done}\")\n    \n    if done:\n        print(\"SUCCESS!\")\n        break\n\nif not done:\n    print(\"FAILED after 80 steps\")\nprint(f\"Final EE: {ee_pos}\")\nPYEOF\necho \"Script written\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":668,"cache_read_input_tokens":102924,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":668},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20edf378-2c29-4db1-9f30-77d38d22d300","uuid":"dde249a2-21b1-4be1-8e69-746c7a798231"}
